Title: Canones apostolorum et conciliorum saeculorum IV. V. VI. VII, pars altera (Bruns)
Creator: The councils of the Gallic and Spanish churches, 4th-7th c. (anonymous conciliar acts)
    (edited by Hermann Theodor Bruns)
Publisher: Berlin, Typis et sumptibus G. Reimeri, 1839
Rights: Public Domain
Rights basis: 1839 publication, out of US copyright by date; Internet Archive item metadata states
    possible-copyright-status NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT, read directly 2026-10-23.
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item canonesapostolo01brungoog
    (archive.org/details/canonesapostolo01brungoog), fetched directly (curl, raw bytes)
    from the item's own _djvu.txt full-text file, 2026-10-23, Library thread (Era III).
Provenance note: Raw, uncorrected djvu OCR (rough). Runs of 2+ spaces collapsed to one; no wording
    changed. Sections: councils in order (Agde, Orange II at raw line ~13557, Epaon,
    Orleans, Macon, Tours II, Clermont II; Braga I-III, Seville I-II, Saragossa, Lerida,
    Valencia, Huesca, Barcelona, Merida, Tarragona, Narbonne). Credit line for a reader-facing page: "Canones apostolorum et conciliorum, pars altera, ed. H. T. Bruns
    (Reimer, 1839)". Whole text republishable.
Content note: The second part of the Bruns canon collection whose first part is vendored as codex-canonum-ecclesiae-africanae_bruns-pars1-1839.txt; it carries the Gallic councils of
    Agde, Orange and Orleans and the Spanish councils outside Toledo. No public-domain
    English exists.

XVn. Ut post mortem episc.opi nuUi subjectorwn liceat de eo 

guidquam detrahere. 
Quia per divinam gratiam in sancta dei ecclesia episco- 
palis ordinatur potestas, dignum est ut sicut lionore et bonitate

ingenuuS; in domo ecclesiae tamen nutritus et ab ecclesiae 
rebus dignitatis gratia praeditus^ juxta quod dignitas ejus ex^ 
egerit^ pro tali excessu excommuuicationis sententia feriendus

discretipnis ^) est gradus^ si major fuerit qui dignitate polleat, 
sex mensibus ab episcopo suo excommunicatus maneat; in- 
ferior tamen aut minima persona discipltnam mereatur, juxta 
quod episcopi sui processerit sententia. Dignum est etenim 
ut cui in vita honor fuit impensus^ post mortem detractionis 
subtrahatur stimulus.

XVHI. De observatione psallendi. 
Itemqne pro reverentia domini M artini vel cultu ac virtute

Btatuta praeceperunt ut ad sextam sex psalmi dicantur cum

quod etiam angelo ostendente didicerunt. Si ad duodecimkm

aqua manducet, et non illi sit altera in illa die ulla refectio; 
et qui hoc facere contempserit, iina hebdomada panem cum
